### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 澳洲 8 月就業人數勁增 3.95 萬人大幅優於預期，但勞動參與率上升至 67.1%，
推升失業率至 4.6%，呈現勞動力擴增但市場邊際降溫跡象。澳洲央行總裁布拉
克日前指出，失業率回升至5%有助於抑制頑固通膨。考量物價長期高於目標中
點，市場預期澳洲央行本週二將重啟緊縮，升息 1 碼至 4.60%，創 2011年底以
來新高。
 瑞士央行（SNB）上週四宣布連續第五次將關鍵政策利率維持在0%不變，符合
市場預期。儘管受油價推升使 8 月通膨微升至 0.8%，但仍處於目標區間，央行
預期中期通膨壓力溫和，現行極度寬鬆立場依然適當。決議公布後瑞郎兌美元跌
至 1.2077。相較全球主要央行相繼緊縮，瑞士央行維持超低利率，策略轉為視情
況調節匯市以穩定瑞郎。
 標普全球公布歐元區 9 月綜合 PMI 初值升至 53.1，創三年新高，製造與服務業
同步擴張，新訂單增速達四年多最快，印證經濟無懼美伊衝突引發的能源成本飆

升，展現超預期韌性。受能源價格傳導加速影響，通膨壓力再度升溫，儘管尚未
浮現薪資二輪效應，但強勁景氣數據促使市場大幅提高歐洲央行提前於10月例
會再度升息的押注。
 美國 9 月綜合 PMI 初值勁升至 58.4，創 62 個月新高；製造業與服務業 PMI 亦
逆勢走強至 57與 58.7，凸顯企業擴張強勁，引發市場對通膨持續性的擔憂。市
場焦點轉向 9 月 30日公布的美國 8 月 PCE、個人消費支出與 Q2 GDP終值，同
日日本亦將發布短觀調查與央行意見摘要。若終端消費延續強勁，將牽動美日央
行政策利率預期。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.655，低點在 31.866，以 31.790作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 2.8分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，台股創新高，外資連續五日買超台股並匯入，推升
台幣最高來到 31.655，不過逢低吸引進口商、政府基金等買盤進場，台幣主要在
31.700 附近震盪；臨近周末，美國 9 月綜合 PMI 由 8 月的 56 走升至 58.4，創下逾
5 年來的新高，加上美伊戰事持續，皆推升市場對後續升息的預期，美元指數升至
101 上方，台幣跳空開低，回貶至 31.800 上方，最低來到 31.866，不過隨後出口商
賣壓出籠，台幣跌勢放緩，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.790。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國經濟表現仍具韌性，9月綜合
PMI 由 8 月的 56 上升至 58.4，企業活動加速至逾 5 年來最強，數據公布後美國 10
年債券殖利率升破 5%，Fed Watch 顯示市場對於聯準會 10 月升息預期升高，有助
於美元指數維持偏強格局，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，台股在創下
新高後回落，而外資也結束連續五日買超的走勢，轉為賣超，短線上資金匯入不利
台幣表現，然而，台灣8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6億美元、年增 71.4%，創歷史新高，
AI 需求帶動出口持續強勁，出口商後續拋匯需求預計將提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，
預估本週台幣區間震盪。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走貶。人民幣中間價在上周連續十天走升，並升破6.7500整數
關口，最高來到 6.7468，帶動人民幣偏升，不過由於美伊戰事持續，市場對於聯準
會升息預期提升，美元指數震盪走強，加上週間公布美國9月綜合 PMI由 8 月的 56
走升至 58.4，創下逾 5 年來的新高，美元指數突破 101 整數關卡，令境外人民幣承
壓，USD/CNH回貶至 6.7200上方，終場境外人民幣收在6.7227，週線貶值283點，
境內人民幣則收在 6.7125，週線貶值 141 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣高檔震盪。就國際市場來看，美中兩國完成新一輪談判，美方
表示雙方同意將原訂 11 月 10 日到期的貿易休戰延長至明年 1 月 10 日，並持續就
關稅、貿易及供應鏈等議題進行協商，降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險，對人民幣形
成一定支撐。不過，美國近期經濟數據仍具韌性，加以聯準會偏鷹立場推升美元，
預計將限制人民幣升值空間；就中國國內市場來看，近期人行持續偏強設定中間價，
帶動人民幣維持偏強走勢，惟美中利差再度擴大，預計將限制人民幣升值的空間；
最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，匯價反彈至 20日均線之上，顯示原先下
行動能暫歇，上方壓力先看向季線 6.7350附近，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣高檔震
盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6900-6.7400

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY先上後下。週初，美國多位聯準會官員鷹派談話，帶動美元指數
持續上行，推升 USD/JPY延續前一週的走勢來到 157上方；週間，受美國 PMI 強
於預期與美債殖利率全面上行帶動，市場重新提高對聯準會後續升息路徑的押注，
帶動 USD/JPY大漲突破 159的關卡；臨近週末，日本首相高市早苗表態日圓被低估
是個問題，使市場提高對政策訊號與潛在干預的敏感度，帶動USD/JPY回吐大部分
漲幅，終場收在 157.26，週線上漲 56點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本財務大臣片山皋月上週
五罕見透露，美國總統川普在美日元首峰會上對日圓過度疲弱表達關切，首相高市
早苗亦回應日圓遭低估確屬問題。儘管近期美元指數維持在 101 上方，但在日本政
府可能干預的陰影下，預計將限縮日圓貶值的空間。二、美國9 月綜合 PMI產出指
數創逾五年新高，疊加美伊戰事推升油價突破100美元與柴油大漲，通膨壓力劇增。
美聯儲理事巴爾表態可能需再升息兩次，芝加哥聯儲古爾斯比也示警能源衝擊具持
續性，推升市場押注 10 月升息機率升至六成，預計將支撐 USD/JPY 走勢。三、就
技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY連日上漲一度站回年線但隨即失守，目前依舊承
壓於季線及年線下方，不過各天期均線趨平，且下方尚有月線支撐，短線尚無明確
的方向。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：155.30-159.30 
前週交易區間 
156.56 159.03

### Page 6

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，歐元延續前一週偏弱的走勢，加上聯準會官員鷹
派發言，導致歐元跌破 1.1450的重要關卡；週間，儘管油價自高檔回落，但市場解
讀為能源成本仍處高位、難以迅速回落，推升對美國實質利率與利差優勢的預期。
加上歐洲在能源依賴與成長動能較弱下，相對於美國更難承受長期高利率環境，歐
元最低來到 1.1358；臨近週末，歐元跌深反彈，終場收在1.1391，週線下跌 91點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行首席經濟學家連
恩示警，第二波能源價格飆升將推升通膨，預計至2027年中期始回落至目標水準。
德國央行月報指出，受中東衝突、低天然氣庫存及明年醫療改革影響，通膨將居高
不下；夏季雖因出口疲弱與乾旱致增長趨緩，但在製造業回暖與財政支出挹注下，
估年底經濟有望重啟復甦，預計將提供歐元一定的支撐。二、美聯儲官員接連釋放
強烈鷹派信號，聖路易聯儲總裁穆薩萊姆直言當前 3.75%-4.00%利率仍偏寬鬆，呼
籲盡早收緊；芝加哥聯儲總裁古爾斯比亦警告，AI投資繁榮引發需求過熱，疊加持
續性供應衝擊，可能迫使美聯儲加速且更積極地升息。目前Fedwatch顯示預期明年
4 月前可能再升息三次，將不利歐元表現。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
在各天期均線下方，且接連失守1.15及 1.14整數關卡，空方動能較佔優勢。綜合以
上，預估 EUR/USD本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1300-1.1450 
前週交易區間 
1.1358 1.1495

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.09.21-115.09.24 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.804% ~ 0.830%  
30天期短票利率   1.65% ~ 1.69%  
公債－10 年期-A15109     1.95%  
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.84%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，本周央行持續每日減發定存單，令市場資金充裕，帶動短
率靠向區間低位。 
週二央行標售 2 年期定存單 250 億元，加權平均得標利率為 1.638%，略低於
市場預期，且較上次標售結果下跌 2.5個基本點。 
週四隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.804%~0.830%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.65%~1.69%，利率較上週下跌 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109 及 5 年期指標券 A15108 均未成
交，週四等殖系統參考報價中價分別為 1.95%、1.84%。 
台股排擠效應、國際債市走勢偏空，加上油價續處高檔，通膨潛在威脅仍存，
12 月升息可能性未能完全排除，市場氣氛續偏觀望，預期台債殖利率將維持
高檔區間盤整 ， 預估5年券區間為 1.84%~1.90%，10年券區間為 1.95%~2.0%。

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス 〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9番 1号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー

2026年 10月 30日 全7頁

Indicators Update 
2026年 9月鉱工業生産 
自動車工業が挽回生産で持ち直し、生産全体は3カ月ぶりに上昇

経済調査部 エコノミスト ビリング 安奈

[要約] 
◼ 2026年 9月の生産指数は前月比＋2.2％と、コンセンサス（同＋1.2％、Bloomberg調
査）を上回り、3 カ月ぶりに上昇した。自動車工業は挽回生産で持ち直したほか、AI
関連需要で生産用機械工業なども増産し、生産全体を押し上げた。経済産業省は基調
判断を「一進一退」に据え置いた。 
◼ 先行きの生産指数は緩やかに上昇する見込み。AI関連需要などが引き続き国内生産を
押し上げよう。ただし、金利高による海外需要の下振れなどが生産を下押しするリス
クには注意が必要だ。 
◼ 2026年 11月 9日に公表予定の 9月分の景気動向指数は、先行 CIが前月差▲0.9ptの
117.1、一致CIが同＋0.7ptの 119.5と予想する。この予測値に基づくと、9月の基調
判断は機械的に「改善」に据え置かれる。

### Page 2

1

2 / 7

【生産】自動車工業は挽回生産で持ち直し、AI関連需要で生産用機械工業も増産 
2026年 9月の生産指数は前月比＋2.2％と、コンセンサス（同＋1.2％、Bloomberg調査）を
上回り、3カ月ぶりに上昇した。経済産業省は基調判断を「一進一退」に据え置いた。 
生産指数を業種別に見ると、15業種中 8業種が前月から上昇した。自動車工業（前月比＋
5.5％）は 2カ月ぶりに上昇した。品目別では、普通乗用車（同＋3.3％）や駆動伝導・操縦装
置部品（同＋7.0％）などが増産となった。前月は熊本地震や台風による工場稼働停止や部品
供給の停滞を受けて生産が落ち込んだが、9 月は生産体制がおおむね正常化したことで挽回生
産が進み、自動車工業の生産は持ち直した。また、生産用機械工業（同＋3.4％）は4カ月連続
で上昇した。品目別では、AI関連製品である半導体製造装置（同＋13.8％）のほか、化学機械
（同＋34.9％）などが増産となった。経済産業省「鉱工業出荷内訳表」によると、生産用機械
工業は国内向け（同＋7.8％）が好調だった。汎用・業務用機械工業（同＋4.8％）では、コン
ベヤ（同＋142.0％）や運搬用クレーン（同＋35.5％）が増産となり、2カ月ぶりの上昇となっ
た。一方、鉄鋼・非鉄金属工業（前月比▲1.6％）や窯業・土石製品工業（同▲1.9％）など 7
業種は前月から低下した。鉄鋼・非鉄金属工業では亜鉛めっき鋼板（同▲10.0％）などが、窯
業・土石製品工業ではガラス繊維（短繊維）（同▲6.3％）などが減産となった。 
財別では、生産財（前月比＋1.0％）、耐久消費財（同＋5.3％）、非耐久消費財（同＋0.9％）、
建設財（同＋2.5％）、資本財（除．輸送機械） （同＋1.0％）のいずれも増産となった。設備投
資の関連指標である資本財（除．輸送機械）はAI需要を背景に大幅な増産が見込まれる。

【出荷・在庫】出荷指数は自動車工業などを中心に2カ月ぶりに上昇 
2026年 9月の出荷指数は前月比＋0.6％と 2カ月ぶりに上昇した。業種別では、自動車工業
（同＋2.3％）や汎用・業務用機械工業（同＋5.5％）など 15業種中 8業種が上昇した。財別に
見ると、非耐久消費財と資本財（除．輸送機械）、建設財は低下した一方、耐久消費財と生産
財は上昇した。在庫指数は同＋2.5％、在庫率指数は同＋2.0％だった。

【先行き】生産指数は緩やかに上昇する見込み 
先行きの生産指数は、緩やかな上昇基調を辿るとみている。AI関連需要などが引き続き国内
生産の押し上げ要因となる。ただし、世界的な金利高、特に米国の金利高による海外需要の下
振れなどが生産を下押しするリスクには注意が必要だ。 
AI関連財の拡大は 2024年から日本国内の生産を押し上げてきており、今後も生産の押し上
げ要因になると見込まれる1。26年 8月の世界半導体販売額（3カ月平均）を見ると、前年比＋
144.3％と強い伸びが続いている。米国の販売額（同＋180.9％）がけん引する中、日本（同＋
62.3％）2も力強さを示している。また、日本半導体製造装置協会によると、26年9月の半導体
製造装置販売高（3カ月平均）は同＋48.2％と高い伸びとなった3。AI関連を中心とする旺盛な
半導体需要が反映されているとみられる。ただし、米国の金利高でAI関連需要が下振れする可
能性には注意が必要だ。高金利下で米企業の資金調達負担が増え、多額の資金を必要とする AI
関連投資が鈍化すれば4、日本の生産も輸出減を通して下押しされるリスクがある。 
なお、中東情勢による悪影響は引き続き注視する必要がある。米国の中間選挙後に対イラン
攻撃が再開される可能性も報じられており5、原油供給をめぐる不安は引き続き残る。一方、今
年 2月末に始まった中東危機を受け、ホルムズ海峡の輸送停滞への対応として政府が国家備蓄
原油を放出して以降、初めて買い戻しが実施された6。代替調達が進み、国家備蓄原油の取り崩
しが一服し、備蓄の補充が始まったことは好材料だ。ただし、情勢が再び悪化すれば、原材料
不足を通じて生産が下押しされる可能性がある。 
製造工業生産予測調査では、10月の生産指数は前月比＋5.1％と見込まれる。業種別では 11
業種中 9業種が上昇する見通しだ。電子部品・デバイス工業（同＋16.6％）で集積回路などの
増産が計画されているほか、生産用機械工業（同＋15.5％）では半導体・フラットパネルディ
スプレイ製造装置など、AI関連製品の増産が全体を押し上げる見通しだ。生産指数全体の計画
のバイアスを補正した試算値（最頻値）7で見ると、10月は同＋3.9％と上昇が見込まれる。 
11月の生産指数は前月比＋0.8％と見込まれている。業種別では 11業種中 7業種が上昇する
見通しだ。輸送機械工業（同＋4.3％）では乗用車など、汎用・業務用機械工業（同＋4.8％）
では計測・分析機器などの増産が見込まれている。

1 詳細は、神田慶司・秋元虹輝・菊池慈陽・横田凱「日本経済見通し：2026年6月」（大和総研レポート、
2026年6月23日）を参照。 
2 World Semiconductor Trade Statistics “Historical Billings Report”（最終閲覧日：2026年10月 30
日）

（2026年10月 23日） 
4 詳細は、矢作大祐・藤原翼「米国経済見通し CoreWeave問題をどうみるか」（大和総研レポート、2026年
10月22日）を参照。 
5 「トランプ政権が湾岸諸国に対イラン攻撃への準備を要請 米中間選挙後に最終判断か」（産経新聞、2026

28日）

【26年9月景気動向指数】先行CIは低下、一致CIは上昇を見込む 
鉱工業指数の結果を受けて、2026年 11月 9日に公表予定の 9月分の景気動向指数は、先行
CIが前月差▲0.9ptの 117.1、一致 CIが同＋0.7ptの 119.5と予想する（図表 4） 。先行CIで
は構成指標のうち、鉱工業用生産財在庫率指数（逆サイクル）や最終需要財在庫率指数（逆サ
イクル）、東証株価指数などが前月から悪化した。一致 CIでは構成指標のうち、生産指数（鉱
工業）や鉱工業用生産財出荷指数、有効求人倍率（除学卒）などが改善した。この予測値に基
づくと、2026年9月の基調判断は機械的に「改善」に据え置かれる。

## Document 109: 261027_ml_powertech

Source PDF: all report pdf/261027_ml_powertech.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 18

### Page 1

Powertech Technology

Up-cycle level of earnings to continue; 
reiterate Buy

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 500.00 TWD | Price: 254.50 TWD

Reiterate Buy on above-trend growth and margins 
We believe Powertech Technology Inc. (PTI) can continue to benefit from the AI boom 
and an upturn in the memory chip industry. 3Q results clearly confirm record-high 
revenue (NT$25bn; up 27% YoY) and an above-trend gross margin (24%; normal high 
teen% or 20% range). 4Q guidance (for packaging/testing businesses) also remained 
bullish: DRAM – tight supply vs strong demand; NAND – order increase for more value-
added NAND and enterprise SSD; and logic – mix improvement with FC-BGA and price 
hike (following cost increase). Key customers are Micron, Kioxia, Solidigm (owned by SK 
Hynix) and various Greater China logic chip makers that require outsourced packaging 
and testing services. As we have already assumed up-cycle earnings level for 2027-28, 
our EPS estimates remain largely unchanged. We maintain our PO of NT$500 (27x 2027-
28E P/E applied). Reiterate Buy.  
Key takeaways from 3Q results  
Five takeaways from 3Q results: (1) strong execution in newly growing packaging orders 
for high-end memory (low-power / high-speed DRAM, enterprise SSD, 200+ layers 
NAND), (2) increasing exposure to more advanced logic (through FC-BGA, etc), (3) a well-
balanced sales mix (DRAM: 20% of total sales; NAND: 28%; SiP/modules 9%; logic 43%), 
(4) notably improved gross margins (limited increase in manufacturing costs vs strong 
top-line growth seen), and (5) solid FCF generation (EBITDA: c.NT$8bn vs capex: 
NT$5bn; expected annual dividend yield: 3-4% range).

Key Metrics      
* For full definitions of iQmethod SM measures, see page 15.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Headquartered in Taiwan, PTI is one of the largest OSAT 
firms, which packages and tests memory and logic chips. It 
has around 18,000 employees. The logic chip-related 
business operates under Greatek Electronics (fully 
consolidated into earnings). Management's new focus areas 
in packaging/testing are PLP, TSV, 3D NAND, low-power 
DRAM, SSD, etc.

Investment Rationale 
Our Buy rating is based on strong earnings growth, driven 
by more value-added memory and even logic packaging 
solutions. 1Q-3Q26 earnings have already hit upcycle levels, 
posting a strong YoY growth (largely driven by NAND/SSD). 
We believe the memory super-cycle and customers' 
ongoing outsourcing trend (Micron, Solidigm/Hynix, Kioxia 
and Greater China's logic chipmakers) using Powertech's 
proven backend solutions should be the key contributors to 
strong earnings growth by 2028.

Exhibit 1: Powertech is accelerating its advanced FOPLP packaging strategy through significant capacity expansion, strategic partnerships (including 
Broadcom), and customer wins such as AMD, positioning itself as a key beneficiary of growing AI semiconductor packaging demand from 2027 onward 
Powertech’s recent news summary during Jul-Aug 2026 
Date Key news Summary 
Aug-2026 FOPLP 
production 
-Powertech plans to start production at the world's first panel-level chip packaging facility for AI chips as soon as early 2027 to make server CPUs, AI 
computing chips and advanced optics 
-Powertech said its advanced FOPLP production line has secured a 100% UTR through 2030 ahead of its scheduled volume productio n in 2027 
-Powertech plans to invest NT$70bn (US$2.2bn) in its FOPLP facility, with installed capacity set to increase from 1,000 panels per months in 2026 
to 5,000 in 2028 
Jul-2026 JV with Broadcom -Powertech reported plans to invest US$400mn to create a new JV with Broadcom in Singapore to cater to customer demand for advanced panel-
based packaging services 
-The JV will concentrate on offering additive finer-geometry redistribution layer (RDL) tech for advanced package substrates  
 AMD business -Powertech reaffirmed that it is on track to start shipping AI chips utilizing its advanced PLP tech for AMD in 2H27, dismissing concern s over delays 
-AMD will be one of Powertech's first customers adopting the FOPLP tech, which is considered to be more cost-effective than the mainstream 
CoWoS tech 
  Capex/capacity -Powertech plans to acquire the entire Philippine unit of ON Semiconductor for US$45mn as part of its efforts to navigate global supply chain 
realignment amid escalating geopolitical risks 
-The transaction will be conducted by its subsidiary, Greatek Electronics in which Powertech holds 43% stake

### Page 13

Price objective basis & risk 
Powertech Technology (PWOGF) 
Our PO of NT$500 is derived from 27x P/E based on 2027-28E EPS. This is 10-20% 
higher than 2024-25 peak levels (low- to mid-20x). Since we expect 50%+ higher EPS in 
2026-28E vs 2024-25 on the back of record-high sales/margin, we believe fair multiples 
should be at least on par with the previous peak level. True, the long-term historical 
average P/E came in the range of 10-20x, but the memory super-cycle and AI boom 
should warrant above-trend earnings growth and consequently over 20x fair P/E. 
Taiwan's OSAT stocks also trade at 20-30x P/E.

Upside risk is mostly related to strong packaging orders from memory chipmakers and 
even logic chip suppliers.

Downside risk is mostly associated with an unexpected memory industry downturn or AI 
bubble (e.g., significant packaging order cut).

Update
Accton Technology Corporation  | Asia Pacific
Read-across from Celestica's
3Q26 Results
We see positive implications for Accton given demand strength
(1.6T migration amid sustaining 800G) and breadth (scale up
opportunities) of ethernet  switches in future.
Celestica (CLS.N, not covered) 3Q26 results:
• 3Q26 revenue of US$5,830mn (+24% QoQ/+83% YoY), non-GAAP operating
margin of 8.5%,  a record high, and non-GAAP EPS of US$3.30 (+30% QoQ/

+109% YoY).
° Higher than guidance and 7%/13% above consensus for revenue/earnings,
respectively.
° Drivers: Stronger CCS revenue (84% of the mix in 3Q26), including
networking and compute/storage growth of 68% and 242% YoY,
respectively, vs. guidance 60% and 190% YoY.
• 4Q26 revenue guided at US$6,275-6,575mn (+10% QoQ/+76% YoY at the
midpoint) vs. consensus at US$6,396mn, management raising 2026 target to
US$21bn for revenue (+69% YoY vs. prior US$20.5bn) and US$11.55 for non-

GAAP EPS  (+91% YoY vs. prior US$11.3).
° Driven by hyperscaler-led 800G demand and ramping 1.6T programs,
along with continued AI compute and storage demand.
• 2027 revenue guided at US$40bn (+90% YoY), non-GAAP OPM at 9.0%
(+0.6ppt YoY) and non-GAAP EPS at US$21.5 (+86% YoY)
° It expects CCS to grow 110% (1.6T/800G on networking and ASIC ramp
on compute/storage) and  ATS at 10%, with revenue skewed to  2H on
multiple large ramps and momentum likely sustaining into  2028.

• Pluggable should remain the mainstream interconnect solution, while CPO
might pick up more meaningfully from 3.2T, per company.
• CLS expects ethernet scale up could represent new opportunities in future.
Our view:
• While the customer profiles are different, we still see positive implications
from Celestica's comments for Accton regarding demand strength (1.6T
migration amid sustaining 800G) and breadth (scale up opportunities) of
ethernet  switches.

• We stay constructive on Accton, and believe   it will continue to benefit from
the secular growth in network switches amid increasing data traffic, spec
upgrades, and a wider customer base as well as the AI accelerator modules
on the initiatives for compute diversity.

More Takeaways
Further comments from Celestica:
• Demand visibility is lengthening and it is now having conversations with customers
for 2029/2030 projects

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Accton Technology Corporation (2345.TW)
Base case, P/E, which we believe better captures the company's earnings growth momentum.
We assign a target P/E of 30x to our 2027 EPS estimate, which we view as justified by an
operating profit CAGR of 48% for 2025-28, based on our estimates. This implies a PEG of
around 0.6, vs. its average of 0.7 over 2019-25, when the stock was trading at a P/E of 22x for
an operating profit CAGR of 32%.

Risks to Upside
n Stronger data center capex
n Faster network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand losing share in AI networks
n Stronger custom AI accelerator growth
n Stronger ODM direct business model adoption
Risks to Downside
n Weaker data center capex
n Slower network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand taking more share in AI networks
n Softer custom AI accelerator growth
n Softer ODM direct business model adoption

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Bull/Bear: Yageo (2327.TW)
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 8

Yageo (2327.TW)
30 October 2026 Citi Research
8
Yageo
Company description
Established in 1977 and listed in 1993, Yageo is a leading supplier of passive
electronic components based in Taiwan. The company offers a wide array of
products including MLCC, chip resistors, magnetics, tantalum, and other
components, aiming to be the total solution provider for its customers. By
capacity, the company currently ranks No. 1 in chip resistor and tantalum and

No. 3 in MLCC and inductor globally. Key customers of Yageo include leading
EMS, ODMs, and distributors. The company also has 29 sales offices, 51
manufacturing sites, and 20 R&D centers in 29 nations worldwide.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Yageo shares. Yageo is widely known as a passive
electronic component supplier. Although passive components are generally
viewed as a commodity, Yageo is no longer a commodity play, in our view,

given the company’s foray into premium markets through M&As, which
should help mitigate the impact of sector cyclicality and order volatility. We
expect a more diversified product portfolio to drive stable earnings and better
margins, while improving utilization in its commodity component business
following the end of inventory digestions should add additional fuel.
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,000, based on 23x average 2027E-28E
earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the company’s earnings

performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market.
Our target multiple of 23x is at the higher end of the trading range since 2022
but in line with global peers average. During this period the company has gone
through the cycles, successfully integrated the acquired business to enhance
its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be

published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

研究員：陳泳如 
kate401124-71795@esunsec.com.tw

法 說 前 瞻               產業：封測 
日月光法說會前財務預估 
4Q26 Full Process 放量帶動公司獲利持續轉佳

| 財報訊息   
日月光即將於10/29(四)舉行法說會，市場普遍預估4Q26營收有望季
增約中高個位數，且毛利率受惠於LEAP業務持續成長挹注ATM獲
利，有望進一步攀升；另關注公司對於2027年LEAP營收、ATM獲
利結構有無進一步更新。 
| 營運現況  
3Q26營收優於公司財測：3Q26營收季增27.8%至2,441.6億元，優於
公司先前財測 (估營收季增 21~22%)，主因為 ATM業務大幅優於預
期，另EMS表現也微幅優於預期，兩者成長動能中， ATM受惠於
AI/HPC需求強勁和封測報價調漲，EMS則因蘋果手機季節性備貨、
記憶體成本轉嫁之挹注。我們預估毛利率21.4%、稅後EPS 5.75元。  
| 營運展望  
Full Process 放量帶動LEAP營收、獲利進一步成長：我們預估本季
營收有望季增4.8%至2,557.6億元，約落在市場共識之下緣，主要成
長動能來自於AMD伺服器CPU Venice帶動Full Process放量，且進一
步改善ATM獲利結構。展望明年，我們觀察：(1)封測產業產能利用
率維持高檔、AI/HPC需求維持強勁，持續支撐封測報價存在漲價空
間，且明年Full Process在AMD伺服器CPU、博通交換器晶片等訂單
挹注下 ， 維持高速成長 ， 預估明年LEAP營收將由今年的37億美元成
長至74億美元以上(公司先前已提到明年LEAP營收將翻倍)；(2)EMS
雖面臨消費性電子需求不彰，但於3Q26漲價後，明年營收將有全年
之漲價貢獻。 
| 簡 易 損 益 表 預 估                        ( 單位：百萬元)

力成 (6239 TT) 
報價續漲

永豐觀點 
封測報價繼續溫和上揚，有利毛利率表現。 
 投資評價與建議 
研究部維持力成買進投資建議：力成 3Q26 獲利略優預期，展望 4Q26 營收和毛
利率皆季增，FoPLP 維持 2027 年年中以前可以出貨的預期，2027 年邏輯產品線
為主要營收動能，預估 2026 年 EPS 13.86 元，2027 年 EPS 22.62 元，維持買進
建議，目標價 340 元(15X 2027 EPS)。

ESG 評析 
力成企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

### Page 2

2

力成為全球第五大封測廠， 以發展記憶體積體電路之封測業務為策略， 原以記憶體
測試業務起家 ，2000 年購入力晶後段設備後開始提供客戶封裝加測試一貫化服務 ， 
2012 年入股超豐電子(2441 TT) 後成為其大股東，並提供邏輯 IC 封裝業務。目前
持有超豐股權約 43%。主要客戶為鎧俠、SK Hynix(Solidigm)、美光、金士頓、南
亞科等。3Q26 業務比重為封裝 67%、測試 24%、SiP/Module 9%；產品比重為
DRAM 20%、Flash 28%、SiP/Module 9%、Logic 43%。AI 相關營收目前主要
來自 LPDDR、eSSD 等相關產品。 
3Q26 財報略優預期：力成 3Q26 EPS 3.55 元，略優預期的 3.48 元，毛利率優於
預期，主因產品組合轉佳、封測報價調漲，目前公司封測UTR 維持滿載，且4Q26
仍有調漲報價空間。 
4Q26 營收毛利率維持雙增：公司展望 4Q26 營收續增，三大產品線都將繼續調漲
報價，毛利率因為封測報價繼續調漲將提升，整體展望方向符合預期，獲利預估因
毛 利 率 優 於 預 期 而 上 修 ， 預 估4Q26 營 收 為275.54 億元 (+8.8%QoQ ，
+28.7%YoY)，毛利率 25.7% ，營業利益率 19.6% ， 稅 後 淨 利35.56 億元
(+36.2%QoQ，+90.8%YoY)，稅後 EPS 4.81 元。

### Page 3

3

FoPLP 進度維持，2027 年邏輯產品線為主要動能：公司主要營收來自 DRAM 和
NAND 封測，並耕耘高階 FCBGA 封裝、FoPLP 等技術已久，2027 年將逐步開花
結果 ， 目前公司在大尺寸晶片封裝已有拿到相關訂單 ， 客戶涵蓋AI 應用晶片業者 、
CSP 等，FoPLP 進度維持 1Q27~2Q27 放量，同樣屬邏輯產品線。公司對 2027
年記憶體市場仍持樂觀態度，表示韓廠產能沒開這麼快， 中國業者的產能則優先供
需中國本土需求仍不夠用，故供需狀況仍看不到反轉跡象。整體研究部認為 2027
年公司動能以邏輯產品為主，記憶體成長幅度相對溫和，預估 2026 年營收為
973.19 億元(+29.9%YoY)，毛利率 23.0%，營業利益率 16.6%，稅後淨利 102.31
億元 (+84.8%YoY)，稅後 EPS 13.86 元， 2027 年 營 收 為1,222.37 億元
(+25.6%YoY)，毛利率 26.3%，營業利益率 20.3%，稅後淨利 167.11 億元
(+63.3%YoY)， 稅後EPS 22.62 元 ， 維持買進建議 ， 目標價340 元(15X 2027 EPS)。

## Document 226: [005380] 현대차(005380) 4분기 반등 기대현대차(005380) 4분기 반등 기대4분기 반등 기대 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005380] 현대차(005380) 4분기 반등 기대현대차(005380) 4분기 반등 기대4분기 반등 기대 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

현대차 (005380)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.16  
4분기 반등 기대

3Q26 Preview 
현대차는 3Q26E 매출액 42.4조원(-9.4% YoY, -13.9% QoQ), 영업이익 2.5조원(-
2.8% YoY, -13.5% QoQ, OPM 5.8%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 각 부문
별 영업이익은 자동차 1.7조원(-2.0% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 4.8%), 금융 5,501억
원(-4.5% YoY, -27.5% QoQ, OPM 6.9%)으로 예상한다. 
생산 정상화 및 신차 확대를 통한 수익성 개선 기대 
현대차는 글로벌 도매 판매 91.8만대(-11.5% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 90.9
만대(-9.6%)를 기록했다. 7~8월에 발생한 파업 영향으로 약 5.5만대 규모의 생산 차
질이 발생했다. 파업 영향으로 HEV 판매 증가율이 1%에 그치면서, HEV 판매 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선 효과도 시장 기대에 미치지 못했다.  
4분기부터는 3분기 파업 영향으로 지연된 생산량 회복 및 신차 확대가 본격화되면서, 
전분기 대비 매출 및 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 동사는 보급형 전기차 아이오닉3의 튀
르키예 생산을 9월부터 시작했으며, 이를 통해 유럽 시장 판매량은 점차 회복될 전망
이다. 또한, 1) HMGMA를 통한 HEV 미국 현지 생산 확대, 2) 제네시스 HEV 모델 순
차적 출시를 통해 내년부터 북미를 중심으로 믹스 개선이 본격화될 전망이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 520,000원으로 하향 
2027년 영업이익은 11.2조원(+10.2%)를 기록할 것으로 예상된다. 원화 약세 지속을 
반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다. 다만, 현재 주가는 
12M Fwd P/E 기준 6.1배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 수준이다. 1) RMAC 
성과 구체화, 2) RA(로봇 생산법인) 착공 등 Physical AI 사업이 구체화될 경우 밸류에
이션 할증이 가능할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

목표주가 (하향) 520,000 원 
현재주가 295,500 원

## Document 244: [012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick_cd366e43

Source PDF: all report pdf/[012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick_cd366e43.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(012450) 
한화에어로스페이스 
2026.10.30

AI로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 1,660,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 760,000원(10/29)

 3Q26 연결 매출액은 6조 943억원(-6.0%yoy), 영업이익은 4,520억원(-
46.2%yoy, OPM 7.4%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센 1조 1,614억원을 하회.  
 지상방산의 손익이 크게 악화. 영업이익은 217억원(OPM 1.7%) 기록. 주요 
납품이 4분기에 집중된 가운데 일회성 비용 약 500억원(대전사업장 복구·
정상화 약 300억원, 국내 사업 충당금 약 160억원)과 환율 영향이 겹침.   
 다만 내용을 살펴보면 수출 사업의 수익성 훼손은 크지 않았다는 판단임.  
 일회성 제외 영업이익은 약 717억원(OPM 5.8%)이며, 일회성 비용은 대전
사업장이 국내 물량을 전담한다는 점에서 전액 국내 귀속으로 추정. 내수 
OPM을 일회성 제외 -5% 내외로 가정하면, 내수 영업이익은 약 -826억원
(일회성 포함 OPM -12.7%), 수출은 1,043억원(OPM 17.6%)으로 추정됨. 
 1Q26 대비로는 국내 마진 하락이, 2Q26 대비로는 수출 물량 감소가 가장 
크게 작용(도표 참고). 국내는 저마진 사업 매출이 늘고 고마진 반복 양산 물
량이 줄며 일시적으로 믹스가 악화. 수출은 폴란드 등 주요 인도가 정체되어 
매출이 감소한 가운데 저수익 사업 중심으로 믹스가 악화된 영향. 
 4Q26부터 회복 구간 진입 전망. 폴란드 K9 2차 실행계약 물량의 현지 인
도가 본격화되며, 회사는 2026년 폴란드 K9 30문 이상, 천무 발사대 60대 
이상(*연초 40대에서 상향) 인도를 계획. 수출입 데이터상으로도 폴란드·루
마니아향 출하가 대규모로 진행 중이며 폴란드향 부속류 수출도 정상적으로 
이어지는 것으로 확인. 일회성 소멸과 고마진 수출 집중으로 수출 OPM은 
30% 내외로 회복할 것. 다만 국내는 정상 가동(2027년 상반기 목표) 전까
지 한 자릿수 초반 마진에 머물 것으로 전망. 
 중장기 수익성 우려도 크지 않다는 판단. 2027~2029년 매출 연 15% 성
장 가이던스는 유지됐고, 내년 폴란드 K9·천무 인도 물량은 모두 올해 대비 
증가할 계획. 폴란드 사업 이후 높아진 수출 마진 레벨은 후속 사업에서도 
유지될 전망. 수주는 중동 지정학 불안으로 지연되고 있으나 대공체계 등 문
의가 이어지고 있어, 2027년부터 중동이 수주의 중심이 될 것으로 기대. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 166만원을 유지함. 3Q26 지상방산 
쇼크는 국내 적자와 수출 물량 공백이 겹친 일시적 결과로, 수출 경쟁력 훼
손으로 보기 어려움. 실적 충격에 따른 주가 조정은 분할 매수 기회로 판단.  
 한화에어로스페이스의 주가는 4월 7일 사상 최고가 153.7만원에서 10월 
29일 76.0만원으로 -51% 조정 받은 상황. 12MF P/E 기준 14.3x, 27E 
P/E는 13.9x로 밸류에이션 관점에서도 매력적인 구간 진입. 그동안 3분기 
실적 우려 등으로 부진했다면, 이제는 실적 바텀아웃과 대형 수주 랠리 속 
다시 한 번 반등을 기록할 차례. 동사를 방산업종 Top-pick으로 유지함.

시가총액(십억원) 39,188

당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

## Document 336: DeepSeek上新模式强化智能体能力：一句话定制AI插件，公开提示词搞定3D可视化到视频编辑

Source PDF: all report pdf/DeepSeek上新模式强化智能体能力：一句话定制AI插件，公开提示词搞定3D可视化到视频编辑.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

以 “ ⼀切皆插件 ” 为核⼼理念的开源智能体框架 DeepSeek Harness 推出 “ 创作者模式 ” ，可⼀
句话⽣成插件，发布 7 组中英⽂演⽰提⽰词，覆盖 3D 建筑展⽰、 Transformer 运算可视化、
应⽤界⾯定制、海报设计、视频编辑等场景。
摘要：
DeepSeek 正在进⼀步将 AI 智能体（ Agent ）的能⼒从执⾏任务拓展到⾃主构建⼯具。
11 ⽉ 2 ⽇周⼀， DeepSeek Harness 团队宣布推出全新的 “ 创作者模式 ” （ Creator Mode ），⽤⼾只
需通过⾃然语⾔描述需求，即可将想法转化为可安装、可使⽤的⾃定义插件，扩展 Harness 的⼯
具、技能和交互界⾯。
DeepSeek 在社交媒体上以 “ 你的 Harness ，由你定义 ” （ Your Harness. You Define It ）为⼝号介
绍这⼀功能，并展⽰了设计⼯作台、沙盒游戏和 3D ⼤语⾔模型可视化⼯具等应⽤⽅向。官⽅同
时发布了 7 组中英⽂演⽰提⽰词，覆盖 3D 建筑展⽰、 Transformer 运算可视化、应⽤界⾯定制、

海报设计、视频编辑等场景。
这⼀更新的重点不只是让 AI ⽣成代码，⽽是试图让⽤⼾通过对话直接创建能够融⼊ Harness 运⾏
环境的功能插件，进⼀步降低定制智能体⼯作流的⻔槛。
从⼀句话⽣成插件，官⽅公布 7 组演⽰案例
从官⽅公布的提⽰词合集来看， Creator Mode 并⾮局限于⽣成简单的⼩⼯具，⽽是试图覆盖从
交互式应⽤、专业⼯作台到数据分析界⾯的多种需求。
7 组演⽰案例分别为：
3D 地标建筑展厅：创建独⽴插件，以 3D 模型展⽰巴黎埃菲尔铁塔、中国⻓城、紫禁城等著名
建筑。
Transformer 三维可视化：制作交互式 3D 演⽰游戏，帮助⽤⼾理解 Transformer 内部的计算过
程。
应⽤像素⻛界⾯：允许⽤⼾切换整个 Harness 应⽤的视觉⻛格，提供五种⻛格选择，其中包
括像素⻛。
海报设计⼯作台：在会话中直接调⽤设计插件，⽀持⾃定义海报标题、正⽂和背景 Shader 颜
⾊。
视频编辑⼯作台：提供类似专业剪辑软件的深⾊界⾯，⽀持多视频上传、预览、时间轴剪辑

和导出，还可以直接通过⾃然语⾔指令完成导⼊和编辑。
论⽂阅读器：⽀持上传论⽂并保留原始排版、字体和图⽚，⽤⼾选中⽂字后，可以进⾏⾼
亮、添加笔记，或直接在对话中提问。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

会话统计⼯具：基于⽤⼾真实对话数据，展⽰ Token 总消耗、会话数量、成本预算、 Token 使
⽤时间线、单次会话⽤量及 CPU 活动和利⽤率。
这 7 组提⽰词的共同点是，⽤⼾不必先掌握插件开发流程，⽽是可以直接描述希望实现的功能、
界⾯和交互⽅式，由 Harness 尝试将其转化为插件。
不过， DeepSeek 也在官⽅提⽰词合集中特别说明，部分演⽰插件经过了多轮对话调整，因此仅
凭⼀条提⽰词，不⼀定能够完全复现视频中的最终效果。这意味着 Creator Mode 降低的是开发
和定制的起点⻔槛，并不意味着复杂应⽤已经能够通过⼀次指令稳定完成。
从 “ ⼀切皆插件 ” 到⽤⼾⾃主扩展
Creator Mode 背后，是 DeepSeek Harness 此前确⽴的插件化架构。
DeepSeek Harness 是⼀款⾯向开发者和专业⽤⼾的开源智能体框架，基于 Cordis 构建，其核⼼
理念是 “ ⼀切皆插件 ” （ Everything is a plugin ）。模型、⼯具、技能以及界⾯等能⼒均可以通过插

件进⾏扩展和组合，⽽不是被固定在单⼀的软件功能框架内。
DeepSeek 官⽹⽬前将 Harness 定位为⾯向全球开放测试的开源产品，⽀持桌⾯应⽤和⽹⻚端运
⾏。除了编写代码、分析数据、整理⽂件和研究资料，⽤⼾还可以安装现有插件，或者在
Creator Mode 中通过对话创建⾃⼰的插件，以扩展⼯具、技能和交互界⾯。
从官⽅展⽰的实际开发过程来看，⽤⼾提出创建番茄时钟插件的需求后， Harness 会加载 Cordis
插件开发技能，检查当前运⾏环境和可⽤接⼝，编写插件配置及代码，再调⽤插件管理⼯具安
装，并进⼀步检查插件是否正常运⾏。官⽅演⽰中，⼀个悬浮式番茄时钟插件从开发到安装验
证耗时约 5 分 24 秒。
这也体现出 Creator Mode 与普通聊天式代码⽣成的区别：它不只是输出⼀段代码或⼀个独⽴⽹
⻚，⽽是试图让智能体直接参与⾃⾝运⾏环境的扩展，将⽣成的功能接⼊ Harness 现有的插件体
系。
AI 智能体竞争从模型能⼒延伸⾄⼯具⽣态
Creator Mode 的推出，也反映出 AI ⾏业竞争重⼼的进⼀步变化：随着⼤模型逐渐具备更强的代

码⽣成和⼯具调⽤能⼒，如何让智能体在真实⼯作环境中持续执⾏任务、调⽤⼯具并扩展⾃⾝
能⼒，正在成为新的竞争⽅向。
据此前报道， DeepSeek 今年早些时候组建 Harness 团队，重点开发能够与美国 AI 公司的编程智
能体竞争的产品。 Anthropic 的 Claude Code 等产品，正是这⼀市场的重要竞争者。 DeepSeek 希
望通过 Harness 进⼀步拓展 AI 智能体在代码开发和专业⼯作中的应⽤。
从这个⻆度看， Creator Mode 并⾮⼀次单纯的界⾯升级，⽽是 DeepSeek 在智能体产品⽣态上的
进⼀步布局。
传统 AI 编程⼯具主要解决的是如何让模型帮助⽤⼾编写代码、修改程序，⽽ Creator Mode 试图
进⼀步解决如何让⽤⼾利⽤ AI 直接扩展智能体本⾝的能⼒。⽤⼾既可以让 Harness 完成⼀项任
务，也可以要求它创建⼀个能够反复使⽤的新⼯具，甚⾄定制整个应⽤的交互界⾯。
这种模式如果能够成熟，意味着 AI 智能体的产品边界将不再完全由开发团队预先设定，⽽是可以

随着⽤⼾需求不断扩展。对 DeepSeek ⽽⾔，开放插件开发也有助于吸引更多开发者参与⽣态建

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

不过，插件⾃动⽣成能否真正转化为稳定的⽣产⼒⼯具，仍有待进⼀步验证。复杂插件涉及代
码正确性、系统兼容性、数据权限和运⾏安全等问题，⽣成后能否稳定运⾏、能否安全调⽤⽤

均已具备成熟的⽣产环境可靠性。

使⽤⼯具，进⼀步转向让模型帮助⽤⼾创造⼯具。  如果这⼀模式能够形成开发者与⽤⼾共同参

与的插件⽣态， Harness 就有机会从⼀款开源智能体框架，逐步发展为可由⽤⼾⾃主扩展的 AI ⼯
作平台。

## Document 360: Following the oil shock, El Niño threatens to hurt macroeconomic resilience in Sub-Saharan Africa further (2026-10-21)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Following the oil shock, El Niño threatens to hurt macroeconomic resilience in Sub-Saharan Africa further (2026-10-21).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

21 October 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
FOLLOWING THE OIL SHOCK, EL NIÑO THREATENS TO HURT MACROECONOMIC RESILIENCE IN SUB-SAHARAN
AFRICA FURTHER
Lucas Plé
Seven months after the start of the war in the Middle East, which has led to a sharp rise in oil prices, sub-Saharan Africa is holding up well
overall. The deterioration in external accounts and the rise in inflation are more contained than in 2022, during the last oil shock. However,
this resilience is fragile and is eroding as the conflict drags on. Furthermore, an exceptional El Niño event could significantly worsen the

macroeconomic environment in 2027 1.
Economic growth remains robust
For countries with available data, GDP growth held up well in the first half of 2026 2. In mid-October, the IMF revised its regional forecast for
the full year upwards to 4.5% (+0.2 percentage points compared with April), in line with the performance for 2025.
1 El Niño 2026–2027: multiple pressures on prices and supplies
2 1.4% year-on-year (y/y) in South Africa, 4.2% in Nigeria, 5.1% in Kenya, 7% in Angola, 7.3% in Ghana.

Sub-Saharan Africa is highly vulnerable to climate shocks via the agricultural commodities channel

### Page 2

2
The bank for
a changing
world
Chart of the Week / 21 October 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
A mitigated shock on terms of trade
High prices for export commodities (gold, copper, platinum) have helped to cushion the impact on terms of trade and the external
accounts of hydrocarbon-importing countries. For the 10 countries in our sample (see heatmap), the depreciation of their currencies
against the US dollar since last February has been moderate overall (2.3% on average).

Inflation has started to rise again, but it is still being contained so far
In the ten countries in our sample, although inflation is rising again, it is lower year-on-year for the first eight months of 2026 than it was
for the same period in 2022. This is due to:
1. Monetary policies that were still restrictive before the conflict started;
2. Fuel subsidies;
3.   Macroeconomic reforms implemented in recent years (a return to orthodox monetary policy in Nigeria, and efforts towards fiscal

consolidation in many countries);
4. Moderate currency depreciation since February.
As a result, few central banks have tightened their monetary policy since February (South Africa, Uganda and Tanzania).
However, this resilience is still fragile…
On the one hand, governments that have chosen to cap fuel price rises are seeing their public finances deteriorate. In Senegal, energy
subsidies will go above 3% of GDP in 2026, which is unsustainable. On the other hand, the region’s external accounts are exposed to

volatile global commodity prices and tighter international financing conditions.
… and the macroeconomic environment could quickly worsen in 2027, due to an exceptional El Niño event
The region is particularly vulnerable to climate shocks via the agricultural commodities channel. Given agriculture’s high share in its GDP ,
sub-Saharan Africa is more exposed to downside risks to growth than Latin America, Asia or Central Europe (1 st column of the heatmap).

It is also more exposed than other regions of the world to the inflationary effect of rising food prices, given their higher share in the
consumer basket (2 nd column). Finally, even though its dependence on food imports is lower than in Asia and Europe (3 rd column), the
region is highly vulnerable to rising international food commodity prices due to its fragile external accounts. For several countries in our
sample, foreign exchange reserves cover less than four months’ worth of imports of goods and services (Uganda, Tanzania, Ghana and

Zambia), leaving little room for manoeuvre for absorbing a further external shock.
In addition to shocks via the agricultural commodities channel, other localised risks must be considered. In Southern Africa, El Niño is
expected to intensify droughts and threaten electricity generation as a result, particularly in Zambia (where hydropower accounts
for nearly 80% of domestic electricity generation). In East Africa, the increased flooding that El Niño is expected to cause will threaten

infrastructure – particularly logistics infrastructure – which is already less efficient than the global average 3. These two sources of
vulnerability could also increase pressure on growth, prices and the external accounts.
In conclusion, if oil prices remain high and El Niño is exceptionally severe, the region will struggle to absorb this double shock in 2027.
Despite their status as net exporters of hydrocarbons, Nigeria and Angola are also exposed to a deterioration in the macroeconomic

environment in 2027. Only South Africa appears to be better positioned to absorb the shock.
lucas.ple@bnpparibas.com
3 See the World Bank’s Logistics Performance Index.

## Document 412: Preview of RBNZ October 2026 Monetary Policy Review (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Preview of RBNZ October 2026 Monetary Policy Review (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
Westpac New Zealand Economics
Following on?
• We expect the RBNZ to raise the OCR by 25bps
to 3% at the 28 October Review.
• A vote seems very possible as differences in
view will likely remain among the MPC about
how much the medium-term inflation outlook
has really shifted in recent weeks.
• Data since September show a higher outlook
for year-end inflation and a stronger GDP
starting point, but increased risks to domestic
consumption and residential investment. On

balance, the medium-term inflation picture
will probably be judged to have worsened
slightly given recent movements in energy
prices and the exchange rate.
• Forward guidance will likely leave open
the option to raise the OCR further at the
December Monetary Policy Statement without
presenting it as a sure thing.
• Given the ‘pause’ signal in September, the
press release will likely emphasise the more
concerning short-term inflation picture driven

by the lower exchange rate and higher energy
prices. The tightening in financial conditions
through higher short- and long-term interest
rates, as well as lingering spare capacity, will
be presented as offsets that means the MPC
retains confidence that inflation will return
towards 2% over time.
• We still think a more measured tightening
approach is appropriate from here. Raising the
OCR at every meeting until something ‘breaks’
could be a risky strategy if downside growth

concerns crystalise.
• Nonetheless, the expected hike will bring the
OCR to the top of the RBNZ’s neutral range
more quickly. We now think the OCR will hit
3.5% a meeting earlier in February. Thereafter
we expect further 25bp hikes at the June and
September 2027 meetings, lifting the OCR to a
peak of 4%.

RBNZ decision and communication.
We expect the RBNZ to raise the OCR by 25bps to 3% at
the 28 October Review. As there will likely be differences
in perspective about recent developments in energy
prices and the exchange rate, we think it is quite possible
that the decision will be reached after a formal vote,
rather than by consensus.
As the RBNZ will not be presenting new forecasts (aside

perhaps from updated short-term CPI forecasts) we will
not see an updated OCR forecast track. Rather we expect
the RBNZ will aim to leave their options open at the next
meeting in December. As in September, we expect the
RBNZ to note that conditional on the central economic
22 Oct 2026 | Kelly Eckhold, Chief Economist | +64 9 348 9382 | +64 21 786 758 | kelly.eckhold@westpac.co.nz
ECONOMIC BULLETIN
Preview of RBNZ October 2026
Monetary Policy Review

(Wednesday 28 October, NZT 2pm).

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
outlook, the OCR may need to increase further (the
September projection had portrayed an OCR eventually
rising to 3.25%). The RBNZ will emphasise the future OCR
path is not predetermined.
In September, the MPC agreed that further tightening in
policy was likely required. The bulk of the MPC saw upside
risks to the inflation outlook while two members (Conway
and Hansen) thought the risks were balanced. Recent
developments will likely not have made those seeing

upside risks to inflation more comfortable, while the
more dovish MPC members will likely still see the growth
downside risks given continued softness in house prices
and the mounting pressure on real incomes. Indeed, all
MPC members saw some degree of downside risks to
growth which likely remain relevant. All these messages
should remain in the RBNZ’s updated views.
The MPC will likely continue to present their decision as a
gradual evolution of the approach taken since July, where

a higher OCR now is insurance against a much higher OCR
later should upside medium-term inflation risks arise. We
doubt the objective will be to push market interest rates
materially higher.
The exchange rate will likely have greater prominence
at this Review, as it did at the July meeting. The TWI is
just over 3% lower than previously assumed which – if
sustained – will add some inflation pressure over the
next few quarters. We suspect this will not have shifted

the RBNZ’s medium term outlook for inflation. So, the
discussion will seek to link the implications of the lower
exchange rate for short term inflation projections with the
risk this spills over into medium term inflation. We doubt
the RBNZ will be trying to actively support the exchange
rate in any meaningful way, and indeed the lower
exchange rate may be presented as something supportive
of the medium-term growth profile, reducing risks there.
Financial conditions more broadly are likely to receive

some attention. While the exchange rate is lower than
assumed, there have also been marked increases in
wholesale long-term interest rates and (to a lesser extent)
mortgage rates. The impact of higher interest rates on
managing medium term inflation risks will likely be noted.
The RBNZ is likely to continue to emphasise that at this
stage there is little evidence of second-round inflation
impacts coming from the Iran war and the associated rise
in energy prices. We note that most other central banks

are drawing a similar judgement at this point. Today’s CPI
might be consistent with that judgement, although core
inflation measures have increased and will likely be noted
by the more hawkish members of the MPC. We expect the
RBNZ to point to the risks that such persistent inflation
pressures might emerge at some point. But that would
depend on the duration of the oil price shock. We expect
the RBNZ to continue to have the assumption that the
shock dissipates and that energy prices and hence CPI

inflation will be appreciably lower in the medium term.

RBNZ target range
We expect the RBNZ to note the labour market remains
cyclically weak and is not a significant driver of inflation.
Still-weak employment growth compared to labour force

growth, weak job vacancies growth and low levels of
labour market turnover are in line with this view and could
be emphasised.
Finally, the RBNZ is likely to acknowledge a still-solid
global growth outlook but with significant downside risks
looking forward given the Iran war and concerns around
the sustainability of the AI investment cycle.
Implications for Westpac’s forecast profile
for the OCR.
The expected hike will bring the OCR to the top of the

range of the RBNZ’s neutral rate estimates more quickly.
It seems likely that the OCR will hit 3.5% a meeting earlier
now. Beyond the pause that we had previously expected
at next week’s meeting, we had expected the RBNZ would
be looking to slow the pace of OCR increases once it had
reached 3.5%. We retain the latter expectation, so this
earlier hike also implies that the pause will come a little
earlier, after two further 25bp hikes at the December

2026 and February 2027 reviews.
We continue to see further 25bp hikes at the June and
September 2027 meetings – taking the OCR to a peak
of 4.0% – as it’s likely that more restrictive conditions
will be required to push core inflation back sustainably
to 2%. Needless to say, the path of the OCR in 2027 will
depend on the evolution of numerous factors that remain
very uncertain, not least being developments in the
Middle East.
Arguments in favour of a hike.

• The ongoing conflict in the Middle East and related
developments in global energy markets point to upside
risk for headline inflation, at least in the near term.
Westpac’s forecasts for headline inflation are now
at 2.5% or above for most of 2027 now. Upside risks
to core inflation and inflation expectations also may
emerge the longer the oil price remains above $90-
100/bbl.

### Page 3

3
Westpac New Zealand Economics
• The exchange rate is notably weaker in recent months
as global interest rates have been rising, and the NZ
OCR is low compared to Australian and US settings.
Financial markets even envisage further interest rate
increases in Europe given the high levels of inflation
implied by their September adverse scenarios (3.2%
2027 HICP inflation vs 2.5% baseline). If the NZ OCR
doesn’t keep pace with global trends, then the

risk is for more inflation generated by a lower NZD
exchange rate.
• The ‘real’ OCR rate is relatively low – plausibly around
-1 to -2 % based on headline inflation projections.
Based on core CPI levels the real policy rate is likely
negative. An OCR in the 3.25-3.5% range would deliver
positive real rates based on core CPI forecasts.
• The economy has grown above trend in the last year
reflecting strong export sector incomes and the
lagged impact of past low OCR settings. The output

gap is likely still negative but is closing. Relying on a
prolonged negative output gap to bring core inflation
pressures down could be risky.
• The areas of economic strength in the last year are not
yet waning. Agriculture sector returns remain strong,
while global growth forecasts are being revised up as
the AI investment boom proceeds. The construction
sector is picking up, as is the manufacturing sector.
Tourism is strong, supported by an advantageous

exchange rate.
• Market pricing leans strongly towards an OCR hike at
this meeting. It may be less disruptive to go along with
market pricing, especially given the RBNZ’s forecasts
imply two further OCR increases including one by
year-end. Moving now versus six weeks later is of little
consequence from a macro view. A smaller ‘splash’ at
this meeting could be desirable given the proximity to
the election.
Arguments in favour of no change.
• For all the upward pressure on short term inflation,

there is little evidence that the medium-term inflation
outlook has shifted much. Domestic inflation in the
September quarter was 0.3ppts weaker than the RBNZ
had expected, which should be a source of comfort.
In addition, energy and exchange rate inflation
impacts on inflation occur too early for the MPC to
offset and will be what they will be. And measures of
pricing behaviour and inflation expectations in recent
consumer and business surveys seem benign given the

scale of recent energy price increases.
• Firms report pressure on margins and an inability
to set prices high enough to recoup cost increases.
Profitability is struggling which clouds the outlook.
Firms expect better times ahead – perhaps because
they think energy prices will fall – but note tougher
trading conditions now. Hiring still seems modest
albeit positive. It does not appear that hiring is yet
strong enough to reduce the high unemployment rate.

• Housing market trends look quite weak and seem
set to constrain consumer spending and residential
investment. Despite very low mortgage rates in the
last year, house sales have declined and house prices
are flat to falling. It seems unlikely we will see a change
in these weak trends soon – especially if interest
rates continue to rise. The result is we are not seeing
a ‘consumption rich’ recovery and growth is relatively
unbalanced. There could be additional risks to the

sustainability of this recovery should external demand
suddenly weaken for some reason.
How might the election play into the
decision?
While next week’s meeting is close to the General Election
on 7 November, the decision taken by the MPC will not
be politically motivated. However, the election outcome
is close. And should it result in a Labour Party-led
coalition, it seems likely the new government would adopt
the Labour Party’s promise to restore the RBNZ’s dual

mandate. That would require the MPC to weigh the degree
of labour market slack more highly in its deliberations. As
the degree of labour market slack currently seems high,
a new mandate might justify the MPC taking more time
to return inflation to the middle of the target range (our
earlier note discusses this issue). This could figure into
the deliberations of some MPC members if the decision
between leaving the OCR unchanged and raising it now is

viewed as finely balanced.
Scenarios.
Our core scenario, with perhaps around a 60%
probability, is that the RBNZ increases the OCR by 25bp
and signals the December meeting is live but without
committing to a markedly tighter future stance than
presented in September. Alternative scenarios include:
• A hawkish scenario (around a 20% probability) would
see the OCR increase to 3% and have the RBNZ more
strongly emphasise the upside risks to core and

medium-term inflation, leading to the implication that
an upward revision of the RBNZ’s forward OCR profile
is in the offing in December. The downside risks to the
exchange rate could also be emphasised.
• A dovish scenario (around a 20% probability) is one
where the RBNZ leaves the OCR unchanged and signals
that the medium-term outlook for inflation has not
yet changed meaningfully relative to that forecast
in the September meeting, despite the higher near-

term inflation outlook. This would cause the RBNZ to
reiterate its previous forecast, i.e. one 25bp increase in
December and one more hike in 2027 sometime.

### Page 4

4
Westpac New Zealand Economics
Appendix: Key developments since the
September Monetary Policy Statement.
Activity: The economy is coming from a stronger starting
point than the RBNZ assumed in September. GDP rose
by 0.2% in the June quarter, and along with revisions to
earlier quarters, the annual growth rate lifted to 2.6%
against the RBNZ’s forecast of 2.1%. Against this, recent
high-frequency data and the resurgence in fuel prices
presents some downside risk to the RBNZ’s growth

forecasts for the second half of the year. The RBNZ’s
Nowcast estimates of growth have drifted lower in recent
weeks, and while these are not the same as the RBNZ’s
official forecasts, they may be a useful guide as to the
direction of change.
Sentiment-based activity indicators: The NZIER’s
Quarterly Survey of Business Opinion was mixed. Firms
were more optimistic about the coming months, but
recent performance has been more subdued and
continues to fall short of expectations. Hiring and

investment intentions picked up compared to last
quarter, but more firms cited a lack of demand rather
than capacity as their main constraint on growth.
Meanwhile, the monthly PMI surveys have shown ongoing
strength in manufacturing, with more modestly positive
readings for service industries.
Housing market: Housing market conditions have
remained soft. House sales have fallen a further 4%
since July, the average time to sell remains elevated,
and prices have continued to edge lower. The RBNZ had

already assumed a modest decline in prices over 2026,
and the recent performance has been broadly in line with
their assumption.
Labour market: Filled jobs have picked up in the last
two months, having been flat through the first half of this
year. That’s in line with the RBNZ’s forecast of a rise in
employment and a tick down in the unemployment rate
to 5.5% in the September quarter. The QSBO showed
some tightening in the availability of skilled workers and a

lift in hiring intentions. Net migration has been in line with
the RBNZ’s assumptions.
Inflation: Annual inflation slowed to 3.7% in the
September quarter, below the RBNZ’s forecast of
3.9%. The downside surprise was on non-tradables
inflation, while tradables inflation was in line with the
RBNZ’s forecast. Underlying inflation pressures remain
elevated, with the various measures of core inflation
close to or above the top of the RBNZ’s 1-3% target

range. Since the September MPS forecasts were finalised,
oil prices have surged higher again, with petrol prices
setting new record highs. The Dubai crude spot price
is currently around $110/bbl, compared to the RBNZ’s
assumption of an average price of around $84/bbl for the
December quarter.
Inflation expectations: The QSBO showed that firms’
expectations of costs and prices remain elevated, but
lower than they were in the June quarter. ANZ’s monthly

survey showed a small dip in inflation expectations
among both businesses and consumers, although the
survey period may have pre-dated the worst of the
latest resurgence in fuel prices (the next updates will be
released just after the OCR review).
Trade: The June quarter terms of trade were in line with
the RBNZ’s forecast. Prices for New Zealand’s commodity
exports have generally remained firm in recent months,
with a pickup in dairy prices against some easing in beef

and lamb prices.
Financial conditions: Domestic financial conditions have
provided mixed signals since the September MPS. Long-
term interest rates have risen sharply along with global
markets, which the RBNZ may regard as an unwarranted
tightening of financial conditions to the extent that it
hasn’t reflected domestic economic conditions. Most
fixed-term mortgage rates have risen by 5-15 basis points
since the September MPS, but rates for the popular

two-year term have actually fallen a little as lenders
have looked to compete in this space. Meanwhile, the
New Zealand dollar has fallen sharply and the trade-
weighted index is just over 3% below the RBNZ’s assumed
average for the December quarter. If sustained, this will
add to imported inflation pressures over the year ahead.

## Document 415: Quantum dictionary, part 2_ From potential to progress We go beyond the quantum basics to explore what’s next, from emerging applications to the global race for leadership. 2 min r

Source PDF: all report pdf/Quantum dictionary, part 2_ From potential to progress We go beyond the quantum basics to explore what’s next, from emerging applications to the global race for leadership. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-11-01
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

13037420     1

Transformation 
Quantum dictionary, part 2: From potential to progress 
02 November 2026

Key takeaways 
• In the second and final part of our quantum dictionary series, we move beyond the basics to explore where quantum could go 
next. Today's quantum computers remain limited by noise and errors, but their potential grows rapidly as they scale. Emerging 
applications like quantum AI and sensing could eventually unlock new capabilities and significant economic value. 
• Quantum communication could change both how we connect quantum computers and how we protect information. A quantum 
internet could use entanglement to link quantum systems, creating additional computing power, while quantum networks could 
make eavesdropping detectable and offer new ways to securely transmit information. 
• Meanwhile, the race for quantum leadership is already underway. Governments are pursuing greater quantum sovereignty as the 
technology's potential implications for competitiveness, security and defense grow.

Assessing quantum’s performance and progress 
Quantum advantage versus supremacy 
Quantum advantage is reached when a quantum computer can solve a useful, real-world problem faster than a classical 
computer. The problem is, today’s quantum computers are still too noisy, as we discussed in part one of this series, and qubit 
quality isn’t yet high enough to demonstrate quantum advantage.  
Quantum supremacy takes this one step further. It refers to the point when a quantum computer has computational advantage, 
meaning it can solve a problem that a traditional computer couldn’t in any feasible amount of time.  
Quantum scaling 
Quantum scaling is about building bigger, more powerful quantum computers. It involves overcoming the challenges of 
increasing both the number and the quality of qubits. Today, we’re in the noisy intermediate-scale quantum era. Quantum 
computers have tens to a few hundred qubits, but noise and errors still limit what they can do.1 
However, when quantum computers do scale, they do so exponentially. Every additional qubit doubles the number of operations 
the system can perform. A quantum computer with just 10 qubits could perform more than 100 times the operations of a 
classical computer (Exhibit 1). More qubits could therefore mean more operations and, ultimately, more economic value in the 
industries where quantum computing can be put to work, according to BofA Global Research. 
Exhibit 1: A quantum computer with just 10 qubits could perform more than 100 times the operations than classical 
Number of classical versus quantum operations for a given (qu)bit number (log scale)

Source: BofA Global Research

Quantum Benchmarking Initiative 
The Defense Advanced Research Projects Agency’s (DARPA’s) Quantum Benchmarking Initiative (QBI) aims to answer a big 
question: Is it possible to build a commercially relevant quantum computer by 2033? To be clear, QBI is not a competition 
between companies. DARPA evaluates all viable approaches for which funding is available.  
QBI has three stages:  
• Stage A: Start with a concept. At this stage, there is a utility-scale quantum computer concept that has a plausible path to 
realization in the near term. 
• Stage B: Put a plan behind it. This stage involves developing a research and development plan, identifying the associated 
risks and introducing prototypes to overcome them. 
• Stage C: Put it to the test. In the final stage, companies work with the government to verify and validate that the utility-
scale quantum computer concept can be built as intended. 
Exploring emerging quantum applications  
Quantum AI 
According to BofA Global Research, quantum AI brings together two powerful technologies: AI and quantum computing, with the 
potential to improve both the physical and the digital worlds. And the relationship could work both ways. Quantum computing 
could provide some of the compute power needed to train generative AI, while generative AI could help speed up the 
development and testing time of quantum systems.  
AI tools like machine learning (ML) could also make quantum error correction (QEC) more efficient and scalable.2 ML models 
trained on simulated datasets can identify and correct errors faster than traditional methods while using fewer computing 
resources. They can also more accurately predict errors before they happen.3 
Quantum sensing 
Quantum sensing uses the properties of quantum mechanics to measure things like magnetic fields, gravitational forces, time 
and rotation with greater precision than classical instruments (Exhibit 2).4 
Exhibit 2: Quantum sensors use quantum properties to measure physical quantities with unmatched precision 
An infographic of quantum sensing

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

2 Wang, Z. & Tang, H. (2024, December 29). Artificial Intelligence for Quantum Error Correction: A Comprehensive Review. arXiv. 
3 Swayne, M. (2025, January 6). AI for Quantum Error Correction: A Comprehensive Guide to Using Artificial Intelligence to Improve Quantum Error Correction. 
Quantum Insider.  
4 Rehman, M.U. (2026, March 2). Understanding Quantum Sensing and Its Industrial Potential. Quantum Insider.

### Page 3

02 November 2026    3

I NSTI TUTE

So, how does it work? In simple terms, there are three steps. First, a quantum system is prepared in a well-defined initial state, 
for example, by cooling an atom to near absolute zero. Next, the sensor is exposed to whatever is being measured, causing a 
subtle shift in its quantum state. In an atomic magnetometer, for example, a magnetic field changes the energy levels of an 
atom’s electrons, which changes how the atom absorbs or emits light. Finally, the sensor measures how much the quantum state 
has shifted, revealing the strength of the external force.5  
Benefits and challenges 
Compared with quantum computers, quantum sensors face some lower technical barriers. They don’t need nearly as many qubits 
to be useful, entanglement can help but isn’t required for every application, and even short coherence times can produce useful 
measurements.6 The catch? They often require large vacuum chambers, laser systems and magnetic shielding to maintain 
coherence. 
The quantum internet and communications  
Quantum communication 
Quantum communication is the study of how information can be encoded and transmitted between quantum systems over a 
distance (Exhibit 3). It uses properties like superposition and entanglement to enable the secure transmission of information. 
Why does that matter? One of the main goals is to keep information secure from potential eavesdroppers, particularly as more 
powerful quantum computers could pose new challenges for traditional communication methods. The ultimate goal is what some 
call the quantum internet, where quantum signals can move seamlessly across many quantum networks.7 
Exhibit 3: Quantum communication uses quantum states to encode and transmit information 
An infographic of quantum communication

Source: BofA Global Research  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Quantum internet 
Think of the quantum internet as a scalable network that allows two or more quantum devices to exchange information (Exhibit 
4). These connections could span vastly different distances, from within a silicon chip all the way to satellite links.8 
But the quantum internet isn’t meant to replace the internet we use today, nor is it simply a faster version of it. According to 
Cisco, the classical internet can move information between computers, but it is ultimately limited by what those computers can 
compute. Some problems become so complex so quickly that no classical machine can solve them in a useful amount of time.

5 Ibid.  
6 Ibid.  
7 Quantinuum. (2024, July 30). Coming Over the Horizon: Quantum Communication Enters the Mainstream. 
8 Imperial. (n.d.). Quantum Internet.

### Page 4

4 02 November 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

When connection becomes computation 
Here’s what makes the quantum internet different: the connection itself can create new computing power. Using entanglement, 
a quantum network can connect quantum systems into a single, larger computing system that scales exponentially.9 That’s 
fundamentally different from classical computers, which communicate by passing copies of information back and forth. Quantum 
computers connected through a quantum network can instead create a feedback loop of entanglement and a shared space of 
possibilities that neither could produce on its own. 
Different hardware from classical 
Building a quantum internet requires a different set of hardware than the routers and switches that power today’s internet. Key 
pieces include:10   
• Quantum communication channels: These carry quantum signals, typically through fiber-optic cables or satellite links 
using free-space optics. 
• Quantum memory: To keep the network in sync, the system must catch a photon and store its quantum state in a 
stationary atom without destroying it. 
• Quantum repeaters: These help quantum signals travel longer distances by amplifying signals and reducing error rates. 
According to QuEra, the lack of viable quantum repeaters is currently a key limitation to building a global quantum 
network. 
Exhibit 4: A network of quantum computers which could send and receive information encoded in quantum states 
An infographic explaining the quantum internet

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Quantum networking 
Quantum networking is a way of sending information using quantum states. It generally includes three network types: quantum 
key distribution (QKD) for secure communications (more on this below), the quantum internet for distributed quantum 
computing, and quantum sensing networks that use entanglement to improve measurements.  
Classical networks turn information into electrical or optical signals that can be copied or intercepted without the sender or 
receiver knowing. Quantum networks work differently. Because information is transmitted using quantum states, it cannot be

9 Chan, M. (2026, June 1). Quantum internet 101: What it is, and why it matters. Cisco. 
10 QuEra. (n.d.) Quantum Internet.

### Page 5

02 November 2026    5

I NSTI TUTE

intercepted and copied without disturbing the original information. That disturbance makes an eavesdropping attempt 
detectable. 
Evaluating security & strategic risks 
Quantum encryption 
Quantum encryption uses the principles of quantum mechanics, including superposition and entanglement, to encrypt and 
securely transmit data (Exhibit 5). 
One potential risk of quantum computing is that it could eventually break widely used forms of encryption. And while large-scale 
quantum computers are still being developed, organizations may need to start preparing for that possibility today.  
Exhibit 5: Examples include quantum key distribution and post-quantum cryptography 
An infographic illustrating quantum encryption

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Quantum key distribution 
Quantum key distribution (QKD), a type of quantum encryption, uses the laws of quantum physics to create and distribute secure 
keys which prevent data decryption.11 Think of a key as something that locks and unlocks a digital message. Two parties share a 
key to encrypt and decrypt information sent between them. If an eavesdropper tries to obtain the key, the attempt disturbs the 
quantum states, alerting the parties that the key may have been compromised and shouldn’t be used.12 
According to Quantum Insider, QKD generally involves three steps:13 
• Create entangled photons: First, a quantum source, such as a laser and nonlinear crystal or a quantum dot, generates pairs 
of entangled photons. The photons are correlated in ways that classical light cannot replicate, so measurements of one 
reveal information about its partner.  
• Send the photons: The photons travel to different locations through fiber-optic cables or free-space optical channels, 
such as satellite-to-ground or satellite-to-satellite links, while maintaining their quantum correlations. But those 
connections can be lost over long distances, with current systems reaching roughly 100 kilometers, according to Quantum 
Insider.14 Classical networks get around distance limitations with repeaters that receive, amplify and retransmit signals.

11 Toshiba. (n.d.). Quantum Key Distribution (QKD). 
12 QuEra. (n.d.). Quantum Key Distribution. 
13 Rehman, M.U. (2026, March 9). Understanding Quantum Networking and Its Industrial Potential. Quantum Insider. 
14 Ibid.

### Page 6

6 02 November 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

Quantum signals can’t simply be amplified without destroying their quantum properties, so quantum repeaters take a 
different approach. They use entanglement swapping and quantum memory to extend the range of quantum networks. 
• Measure the photons: Finally, the photons are measured at each location. This confirms whether the entanglement 
survived the journey and can reveal whether someone tried to intercept the photons along the way. The results can then 
be used to generate a shared secret key for encrypting classical messages. 
Quantum sovereignty 
Quantum sovereignty is an economic actor’s ability to develop, produce and operate its own quantum technology.  
Quantum is the first new computing platform technology since the arrival of digital computing. And the stakes are high. 
Stakeholders are interested in quantum because of its potential implications for economic competitiveness, security, supply 
chain resilience and defense. As a result, some may pursue “sovereign quantum,” meaning quantum capabilities are owned and 
operated by a particular nation. That could involve investing in quantum technology, building domestic expertise or even export 
controls to limit other stakeholders’ ability to develop their own sovereign capabilities.  
The geopolitical race is on 
Government quantum investment has reached at least about $58 billion cumulatively as of 2025, led by Europe at $15.4 billion 
and China at $15.3 billion.  
The competition extends beyond investment. At least nine countries implemented export controls on quantum technology in 
2024. Over the past 20 years, China and the US have led in patent requests filed, but Europe and China have led in the number 
of patents granted. The race for talent is global, too. China has trained the largest share of top quantum researchers at the 
undergraduate level, at roughly one-fourth, while the US is home to the largest share of top talent by employment, at one-third.  
Quantum supply chain 
Building quantum technology requires much more than qubits. A broader supply chain provides the equipment, components and 
materials needed to make quantum systems work, including: 
• Cryogenics: Most types of qubits require some level of extreme cooling, with superconducting qubits among the most 
demanding at roughly 5–10 millikelvin. Keeping qubits this cold helps shield them from environmental noise that can 
cause decoherence. 
• Qubit control electronics: Quantum gates direct qubits by sending them photons. That requires supporting electronics, 
including wiring, room-temperature electronics and amplifiers. For example, analog microwave signals used to read qubits 
must be amplified through several stages before they can be converted into digital signals and analyzed. 
• Photonics: Lasers use light and atoms to help power quantum technology, while other devices can modify properties of 
photons, such as their path and polarization. 
• Foundries: Quantum development also relies on several types of fabrication facilities, including research fabs at national 
research organizations and universities, pre-industry research fabs, independent foundries that manufacture 
semiconductors for third parties, and in-house fabs operated by quantum technology vendors. 
• Raw materials: Quantum systems also depend on materials used in qubits themselves and in supporting components such 
as cables and cryostats. Helium-3 and helium-4 are used in cryostats to maintain very low temperatures, while rare earth 
metals used in quantum technology include yttrium, neodymium, europium, erbium and ytterbium.15 
AI singularity 
AI singularity refers to a hypothetical point when AI evolves to become self-improving and autonomous, surpassing human 
cognitive abilities.16  
A complementary relationship between quantum and AI 
Rather than developing in isolation, AI and quantum computing could ultimately help advance each other. Quantum computing 
may eventually provide some of the compute power needed to train generative AI, while generative AI could help speed up the 
development and testing of quantum systems.

15 Swayne, M. (2025, October 15). Rare-Earth Minerals And Quantum Computing: How Secure Are The Supply Chains? Quantum Insider. 
16 ISB. (2025, March). What is AI Singularity? Explore Its Future Impact.

### Page 7

02 November 2026    7

I NSTI TUTE

That relationship could become increasingly important as the computing power needed to train the largest generative AI models 
continues to grow exponentially. Classical computing may eventually struggle to keep pace, while quantum computing could help 
meet some of that demand because its computing power also scales exponentially. 
By 2045? 2029? Now? 
AI is becoming increasingly powerful and capable, raising a big question: when could it reach or even surpass human intelligence? 
Futurologist Ray Kurzweil expects AI to reach human levels of intelligence by 2029 and technological singularity by 2045. His 
view extends Moore’s Law beyond processing power to other forms of technological progress. 
Under that framework, computing power would continue growing exponentially. By 2045, Kurzweil projects that $1,000 worth of 
computing could perform as many calculations in one second as all human brains combined.

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

Monetary Authority of Singapore (MAS)

We expect another slight increase in the 
SGD NEER policy band slope at the MAS’ 
October review, with no change to the 
band’s width or centre. Import cost 
pressures and resilient growth favour a 
calibrated tightening, rather than a more 
forceful adjustment, in line with the 
global monetary policy stance.

Also due to be released alongside the 
MAS’ policy decision are the 3Q26 
advance GDP estimates, which we 
expect to show another quarter of strong 
growth at 5.4% yoy (1.4% qoq sa), 
compared with 5.9% yoy (1.4% qoq sa) in 
2Q26. Robust economic performance 
continued to be driven by artificial 
intelligence ( AI)-led trade activity, 
supporting the manufacturing and 
wholesale trade sectors, while strength 
in the financial sector underpinned the 
resilience of the modern services cluster. 
This came despite a moderation of the 
transport & storage sector, and 
weakness in consumer -facing food 
services and retail sectors.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Malaysia

We expect Malaysia’s advance GDP 
estimate for 3Q26 to register above -
trend growth of 5.0% yoy, albeit 
moderating from 6.0% yoy  in 2Q26. The 
overall expansion continued to be 
supported by ongoing strength in the 
export-oriented manufacturing sector, 
underpinned by strong  AI-related 
demand for electronics, and sustained 
domestic demand, backed by healthy 
investment and private consumption 
trends. However, contraction in mining 
and weak agricultural activity dragged 
overall growth.

Singapore

We expect firm Singapore non -oil 
domestic exports (NODX) growth of 28.0% 
yoy in September 2026, although 
moderating from August’s exceptional 
surge of 46.2%. Although favourable 
base effects largely faded in September, 
electronics shipments likely remained 
the primary growth driver, underpinned 
by robust AI -related demand amid 
sustained global investment in AI 
infrastructure, while non-electronics 
exports were uneven.

China

Exports growth is expected to remain 
robust at 25.7% y oy in September, 
supported by continued strength in 
electronics demand. Both the official and 
Caixin manufacturing PMIs rose above 
the 50 expansion threshold during the 
month, pointing to improving 
manufacturing activity. External trade 
indicators also stren gthened, with 
average daily deadweight tonnage 
handled at China's 20 major ports 
accelerating from -0.7% yoy in August to 
7.2% in September. Freight activity 
improved as well, as growth in 
international cargo flights picked up 
from -1.4% y oy to 3.6% over the same 
period.

On the price front, factory -gate and raw 
material prices rebounded amid the 
renewed escalation in geopolitical 
tensions. Main raw material purchasing 
price and producer price subPMI 
increased from 56.6 and 50.4 to 60.8 and 
54.0 during the period. However, 
downstream consumer price pressures 
are likely to remain relatively subdued 
under the pricing-band mechanism. As a 
result, CPI inflation is expected to edge 
up from 0.8% y oy in August to 1.0% in 
September. Nevertheless, soft domestic 
demand is expected to continue 
constraining the pace of consumer price 
increases.

India

September inflation is likely to gather 
momentum to 5.7% y/y, partly driven by 
base effects, from 4.8% the month 
before. Food segments continue to 
register gains, with pressures 
broadening to include perishables, 
edible oils, rice, pulses, sugar, etc., 
according to high-frequency data. Under 
non-food, retail pump prices have not 
been adjusted further, though other fuel 
products, such as non -subsidised LPG, 
diesel and ATF (still up by double digits 
y/y, though slower than in August), are 
up on the year. Core  inflation is also 
likely to tick up, aligning with the central 
bank's view that price risks are no longer 
benign. Pressures have built up due to 
the overhang of an uneven and sub -par 
monsoon, drought conditions in parts of 
the country, and elevated oil pr ices. In 
the face of higher headline prints, we 
expect the RBI to keep the door open for 
another rate hike in December 2026.
